# TDC Procurement and Inventory Gap Implementation Tracker

Last updated: 2026-08-03

## Purpose

This tracker is the delivery ledger for closing the gaps between the current ERP implementation and the requirements in the client-issued `TDC SRS Procurement ERP.docx`, reconciled with the earlier detailed `Procurement ERP.docx` questionnaire completed by the Head of Procurement of TDC Ghana Ltd.

The source document covers more than purchasing. It defines the complete procurement and inventory operating model: governance, annual planning, requisitions, sourcing, tendering, supplier management, frameworks, contracts, purchase orders, receiving, accounts payable matching, stores operations, reporting, audit, integrations, migration, training, and go-live success measures.

## Source Of Truth And Boundaries

- Primary client acceptance source: `C:\Users\micha\Downloads\Requirement Documents\TDC SRS Procurement ERP.docx`
- Detailed business source: `C:\Users\micha\Downloads\Questionnairs and SOPS\Questionnairs and SOPS\Procurement\Procurement ERP.docx`
- Source precedence: the client SRS is the minimum contractual acceptance baseline. The questionnaire supplies additional operating detail. A conflict must be recorded as a scope/configuration decision and approved by TDC; neither source may be silently discarded.
- Legal references stated by TDC: Act 663, Act 914, Act 1139, L.I. 2516, Act 921, PPA/GHANEPS obligations, and TDC internal procurement policy.
- Current implementation evidence: live entities, DTOs, services, controllers, workflow adapters, migrations, frontend routes, and existing repository trackers.
- This tracker does not independently certify the legal interpretation of the stated Acts or thresholds. TDC Procurement, Legal, Finance, Internal Audit, and the relevant PPA authority must approve the final policy configuration.
- The tracker covers both Procurement and Inventory because TDC treats requisition-to-payment, stores, stock valuation, disposal, and audit as one controlled lifecycle.

## Delivery Rule

A slice may be marked `Done` only when all applicable parts are complete:

1. Backend domain model and business rules.
2. Database migration and data backfill.
3. API endpoints and authorization.
4. Frontend routes, controls, validation, and status visibility.
5. Notifications, audit events, and reports where required.
6. Automated tests for happy paths, hard stops, overrides, SOD, and tenant isolation.
7. Migration applied to the test database.
8. Browser and API smoke verification.
9. Tracker evidence updated with commands, routes, and test results.

No requirement is complete merely because a field, entity, page, or configurable workflow exists. TDC requires enforced end-to-end behavior.

## Status Legend

| Status | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| Verified baseline | Current implementation has material capability confirmed in source; final TDC acceptance is still required. |
| Partial | Useful capability exists, but required controls or workflow steps are incomplete or bypassable. |
| Gap | Required capability or enforcement is absent. |
| Configuration pending | Implementation can proceed using configurable, effective-dated policy fields; approved values are required before the TDC profile is published for UAT or production. |
| Not started | Approved implementation task has not started. |
| In progress | Implementation is actively being changed and is not yet acceptance-ready. |
| Done | Full delivery rule above has passed and evidence is recorded. |
| Blocked | Work cannot safely continue until a named dependency or policy decision is resolved. |

## Honest Readiness Statement

The requirements are sufficiently understood to design and implement the full solution. The decisions in `DEC-*` are mostly configuration inputs, not implementation blockers. Development should proceed with effective-dated settings, draft and published policy versions, configurable workflow stages, role mappings, evidence rules, and safe validation defaults.

Approved TDC values are required before the corresponding policy profile, workflow, integration mapping, or cutover plan is published for UAT or production. The document contains several incomplete or competing threshold statements, so those values must not be hard-coded or silently assumed.

The current ERP has broad transaction coverage. The main delivery risk is not missing screens; it is bypassable controls between screens. The implementation must therefore prioritize the policy engine, hard stops, SOD, source-document lineage, matching, and audit before adding more presentation features.

The 2026-07-17 revalidation found that the earlier tracker covers the SRS materially and is broader than the 40-item client traceability matrix. It nevertheless required explicit additions for GRN/MRN semantics, controlled supplier/master-data changes, first-class Document Management integration, HR/identity synchronization, complete migration reference data, measurable non-functional acceptance, and direct SRS-to-task traceability. Those additions are incorporated below.

## Configurable Business Decisions And Release Gates

These decisions do not stop implementation unless a task depends on a specific external file or service. The system should support them through configuration. An unresolved value blocks only publication of the affected TDC configuration to UAT or production.

| ID | Priority | Decision class | Configuration or confirmation required | Implementation treatment and release effect | Owner |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DEC-001 | P0 | Configuration input | Confirm the legally applicable procurement method thresholds for Goods, Works, Technical Services, and Consultancy Services. | Build an effective-dated method-threshold table now. Seed draft values from the questionnaire, clearly marked unapproved. Final confirmation is required before publishing the TDC policy profile. | TDC Procurement + Legal/PPA |
| DEC-002 | P0 | Configuration input | Confirm the approval authority matrix and whether the stated GHS 800,000 / 3,300,000 / 8,000,000 / 54,000,000 limits are current and inclusive at boundaries. | Build configurable lower/upper bounds, boundary inclusivity, currency, authority, escalation, and effective dates. Final values gate threshold-routing UAT. | TDC Procurement + Legal/PPA |
| DEC-003 | P0 | Workflow configuration | Confirm the exact approval sequence for every PR. | Use the existing workflow designer plus policy-selected workflow definitions. Department, Procurement, Finance, MD, and other stages remain configurable. Final sequence gates PR workflow UAT, not development. | MD + Procurement + Finance |
| DEC-004 | P0 | Workflow and role configuration | Confirm which transactions require ETC, Board, Central Tender Review Committee, PPA, Legal, Finance, and Internal Audit approval or observation. | Build configurable authority stages, committee roles, quorum, observer, evidence, escalation, and amount/category conditions. Final mappings gate authority-routing UAT. | TDC Procurement + Legal/Internal Audit |
| DEC-005 | P0 | Configuration input | Confirm petty-purchase limits and when evaluation may be waived. | Build configurable petty thresholds, waiver eligibility, required justification/evidence, approver, and expiry. Unapproved waiver rules cannot be published. | TDC Procurement + Finance |
| DEC-006 | P0 | Workflow and evidence configuration | Confirm restricted tender and single-source prerequisites, approval authorities, post-award filing, and mandatory PPA references. | Build configurable exception workflows and evidence checklists. Final statutory checklist gates restricted/single-source UAT. | TDC Procurement + Legal/PPA |
| DEC-007 | P1 | Finance configuration | Define whether a supplier-onboarding token is free or paid, together with token price, registration fees, payment methods, receipt numbering, exemptions, refunds, and renewal rules. | Build effective-dated token mode/price, fee schedules, tax, payment channels, exemption rules, numbering, refund, and renewal settings. The token lifetime is application-state driven rather than time-configurable: it remains valid while the application is active and expires only when the application is Approved or Rejected. Final token and fee setup gates supplier-onboarding payment acceptance testing. | Procurement + Finance |
| DEC-008 | P1 | Document and signature configuration | Confirm signature policy: electronic signature, uploaded manual signature evidence, or both, including who may sign each document. | Support per-document signature mode, required signatory roles, signing order, evidence, and verification. Final legal modes gate document-signing UAT. | Legal + ICT + Procurement |
| DEC-009 | P1 | External mapping configuration | Approve GHANEPS phase-one files/templates, reference fields, submission frequencies, and reconciliation ownership. | Build a configurable export/import profile and integration queue now. Exact schemas and acknowledgements gate only the GHANEPS mapping and reconciliation acceptance. | Procurement + ICT/PPA |
| DEC-010 | P1 | Inventory policy configuration | Confirm negative-stock policy and whether any controlled emergency override is permitted. | Build a default hard stop with optional, separately permissioned exception workflow. Final override policy gates inventory-control UAT. | Stores + Finance + Internal Audit |
| DEC-011 | P1 | Risk policy configuration | Confirm supplier AVL annual review process, risk scoring bands, concentration limits, and minimum acceptable performance score. | Build configurable scorecards, bands, review frequency, eligibility actions, and thresholds. Final values gate supplier-risk UAT. | Procurement + Internal Audit |
| DEC-012 | P2 | Deployment release gate | Confirm go-live cutover date, dual-running period, data ownership, and acceptance signatories. | Does not block product implementation. It gates rehearsal scheduling, production migration, cutover, and final go-live approval. | Steering Committee |
| DEC-013 | P0 | Receipt document configuration | Confirm what TDC means by GRN versus MRN, when each document is required, whether one transaction can produce both, and the numbering/signature/template rules. | Build configurable receipt document types and numbering now. Final terminology and templates gate only receipt-document UAT. | Stores + Procurement + Finance |
| DEC-014 | P1 | Non-functional acceptance configuration | Approve measurable availability, peak-load, response-time, backup, RPO/RTO, authentication, usability, and accessibility targets for PR, PO, GRN/MRN, stock issue, and reports. | Build the NFR test profile and observability hooks now. Approved targets gate performance and operational-readiness acceptance. | ICT + Procurement + Stores |

Production device-fleet provisioning and the broader interface contract remain separately mapped to `TDC-0804`/`TDC-0808`. |
| INT-004 | Gap | No agreed Interface Control Document covering direction, frequency, ownership, retries, reconciliation, security, and failure handling. |
| INT-005 | Partial | Procurement has scattered tender, contract, supplier, GRN, invoice, and workflow evidence attachments plus shared file storage, but no single Document Management contract enforces document classification, versioning, access, malware status, retention/legal hold, and retrieval across the full lifecycle. |
| INT-006 | Partial | Users, departments, roles, and workflow identities exist, but the SRS-required HR/identity interface for active/inactive status and approval hierarchy has no accepted synchronization, reconciliation, or failure-handling process. |
| MIG-001 | Gap | No controlled migration suite for supplier records, AVL, items, UOMs, stores, users/roles, cost centres, chart-of-accounts references, open POs, active contracts, stock balances, WAC history, contract payments, and digital tender archives. |
| MIG-002 | Gap | No cleansing workbench for duplicate item codes, incomplete TIN/documents, UOM conflicts, contract gaps, and Stores-versus-Finance variances. |
| MIG-003 | Gap | No cutover reconciliation, signed opening balances, migration exception register, or rollback criteria. |
| CHG-001 | Gap | TDC role-based training materials, supplier training, train-the-trainer plan, and competency sign-off are not prepared. |
| CHG-002 | Gap | Dual-running, phased cutover, support, incident triage, and go-live acceptance plan are not defined. |
| KPI-001 | Gap | Success measures are not implemented as baseline/target KPIs with auditable calculation definitions. |
| NFR-001 | Partial | RBAC, authentication, performance middleware, backup concepts, and general UI validation exist, but the SRS has no approved measurable NFR profile and the repository lacks procurement-specific load, availability, RPO/RTO, restore, usability, accessibility, and peak-period acceptance evidence. |

## Implementation Roadmap

### Phase 0 - Policy Confirmation And Architecture

| Task ID | Priority | Status | Deliverable | Acceptance criteria |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TDC-0001 | P0 | Done | Define configuration schemas for `DEC-001` through `DEC-014`, seed draft TDC profiles, and attach approvals as they arrive. | Every decision supports draft/published state, owner, decision date, effective date, evidence, approved values, and publication protection; unresolved values do not block unrelated development. |
| TDC-0002 | P0 | Done | Create procurement policy, category, method, authority, threshold, evidence, exception, and SOD domain model. | Model supports effective dating, currency, Goods/Works/Services/Consultancy, tenant overrides, and immutable published versions. |
| TDC-0003 | P0 | Done | Build central procurement compliance decision service. | Given category, amount, method, source, user, and date, service returns required authority, evidence, route, hard stops, and explainable rule IDs. |
| TDC-0004 | P0 | Done | Build reusable SOD guard service and policy administration UI. | All six TDC conflicts are blocked at API and hidden/disabled in UI with audited attempted-bypass events. |
| TDC-0005 | P0 | Done | Seed and verify TDC roles, permissions, committees, workflow definitions, and Internal Audit read-only role. | Test users can perform only assigned duties; tenant and warehouse restrictions pass integration tests. |
| TDC-0006 | P0 | Done | Establish procurement event/audit architecture. | Every control decision records actor, role, rule, values, source, result, old/new state, timestamp, correlation ID, and evidence links. |
| TDC-0007 | P0 | Done | Define controlled procurement master-data change policy and reusable maker-checker service. | Supplier, bank, tax, item, category, UOM, warehouse, location, and policy-sensitive changes use configured approval, effective date, before/after values, revalidation, and immutable audit. |

### Phase 1 - APP, Specifications, Budget, And PR Hard Stops

| Task ID | Priority | Status | Deliverable | Acceptance criteria |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TDC-0101 | P0 | Done | Add GHANEPS/PPA APP submission register and timeline. | Published plan tracks export, reference, submitter, submission date, acknowledgement, rejection, resubmission, and evidence. |
| TDC-0102 | P1 | Done | Add standard specification/TOR templates for Goods, Works, and Services. | Template captures purpose, functional/performance, process/materials, dimensions/marking, testing, standards, deliverables, and acceptance criteria. |
| TDC-0103 | P0 | Done | Complete PR DTO/entity/UI linkage to plan item, budget, category, cost centre, project, request type, specification template, and exception. | Required values persist, display, export, and remain auditable. |
| TDC-0104 | P0 | Done | Enforce APP linkage or approved exception before PR submission. | API rejects unlinked PR; UI explains the blocking rule; approved emergency/exception path is traceable. |
| TDC-0105 | P0 | Done | Enforce real-time Finance budget availability and commitment reservation. | PR cannot progress without sufficient approved budget or authorized override; concurrent requests cannot overspend. |
| TDC-0106 | P0 | Done | Route PR approval by confirmed TDC authority matrix. | Department, Procurement, Finance, MD/authority stages and amount/category rules are deterministic and tested at boundaries. |
| TDC-0107 | P0 | Done | Add PR compliance review and sourcing-release gate. | Sourcing is impossible until mandatory fields, APP, budget, specification, approvals, and SOD checks pass. |
| TDC-0108 | P1 | Done | Configure annual procurement calendar and reminders. | APP, mid-year review, cycle count, year-end, renewal, and GHANEPS deadlines generate owned tasks and escalations. |

### Phase 2 - Sourcing, Tender, Evaluation, And Award Controls

| Task ID | Priority | Status | Deliverable | Acceptance criteria |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| TDC-0201 | P0 | Done | Create sourcing-case record from approved PR/plan demand. | A current immutable PR sourcing release creates one tenant-safe, idempotent case whose immutable snapshot preserves the source demand, selected method, exact policy/method/threshold versions, value/currency, authority route, justification, exception/evidence, lots/lines, source requests, actor, lifecycle, and freshness. Authorized API/UI lifecycle, RFQ/tender entry enforcement, audit, concurrency, database guards, retained-history behavior, automation, test-database apply, and browser/API/SQL smoke are verified. |
| TDC-0202 | P0 | Done | Implement automatic method selection and controlled override. | The server derives the recommendation from the exact current tenant policy for all nine modeled methods; accepting or omitting it records `AutomaticRecommendation`. A different method fails closed unless the current exception rule, explicit `procurement.sourcing.approve` capability, independent completed shared workflow, justification, unexpired evidence, approval actors, and SOD requirements all pass. The immutable case/snapshot/control-event lineage records the recommendation, selection basis, exact policy/method/threshold/exception/workflow/evidence references, actors, and reason. Tenant-safe API/UI, migration/SQL guards, authorization and tenant tests, regression/static checks, test-database apply, API/browser/SQL smoke, cleanup, and preservation gates are verified. |
| TDC-0203 | P1 | Done | Complete RFQ statutory workflow. | A current tenant-matched RFQ sourcing case and exact active method rule are revalidated at every irreversible transition. Qualified-supplier minimums, Draft-only issue, sealed and late receipt rules, immutable receipt/opening registers, officer/observer signatures, bid securities, evidence and integrity hashes, evaluation/SOD, configured shared-workflow approval, and server-derived LPO/PO handoff are enforced end to end. Tenant-safe API/UI, explicit authorization, append-only audit/evidence, focused migrations and SQL guards, automation, test-database apply, API/browser/SQL smoke, cleanup, preservation, and retained sourcing-history gates are verified. |
| TDC-0204 | P1 | Done | Complete NCT and ICT workflows. | Current tenant NCT/ICT sourcing-case, release, method-rule, authority-route, amount, and currency lineage is revalidated at every irreversible transition. Approved advertisement/document version, exact paid-or-free issue/sale, sealed and late submission receipts, signed public opening, separate technical/financial evaluation with SOD, exact shared-workflow authority/PPA approval, approved recommendation, eligible-supplier award, executed contract, bidder acceptance, evidence, hashes, and `DEC-001` through `DEC-014` audit records are enforced. Tenant-safe API, authorization, dedicated history-first UI, migration/SQL guards, automation, test-database apply, API/browser/SQL smoke, cleanup, preservation, and no PO/receiving/inventory behavior change are verified. |
| TDC-0205 | P1 | Done | Complete restricted and single-source workflows. | Current tenant Restricted Tendering/Single Source sourcing-case, release, method-rule, exception-rule, authority-route, amount, currency, supplier, and policy lineage is revalidated at every irreversible transition. Method-specific supplier counts, statutory justification, mandatory verified `DEC-006` evidence, exact Board/MD/PPA shared-workflow outcome, invitation release, negotiation plan/minutes/outcome, negotiated recommendation, eligible-supplier award, executed contract, supplier acceptance, PPA post-award filing, exception report, immutable hashes, and `DEC-001` through `DEC-014` control events are enforced. Tenant-safe API, explicit authorization/SOD, dedicated history-first UI, focused migration/SQL guards, automation, test-database apply, API/browser/SQL smoke, cleanup, preservation, and no general PO/receiving/inventory behavior change are verified. |
| TDC-0206 | P1 | Done | Complete prequalification workflow and reusable qualified lists. | One tenant-safe exercise captures immutable categories, criteria, exact current Published/effective policy/configuration lineage, workflow version, dates, validity, and hash; advertisement and evidence are controlled; supplier applications are current-identity, category, deadline, completeness, and duplicate checked; evaluators are independent and submit immutable criterion scores/reasons/evidence; only the exact completed shared-workflow outcome can approve or reject; qualified-list entries retain application/evaluation/decision lineage and expire without deletion; deterministic category eligibility rejects missing, rejected, revoked, or expired entries. Explicit authorization/SOD, notifications, `DEC-001` through `DEC-014` control events, tenant-safe API, dedicated history-first frontend, SQL hard stops, automation, test-database apply, API/browser/SQL smoke, cleanup, and unchanged later procurement runtime are verified. |
| TDC-0207 | P1 | Done | Add controlled tender document templates and issuance register. | Reusable tenant-safe template families and independently approved immutable versions retain exact policy/configuration/workflow/evidence lineage; tender/RFQ bindings retain the exact approved version; supplier-validated paid/free recipient issuance and receipts are append-only; governed addenda, recipient acknowledgement, submission-deadline extension, and post-closing bid-validity extension retain before/after values, workflow outcome, evidence, notifications, actors, SOD, concurrency, and `DEC-001` through `DEC-014` history. Direct service/API/SQL bypasses fail closed and dedicated internal/external history-first UI is verified. |
| TDC-0208 | P0 | Done | Enforce committee composition, declarations, quorum, signatures, and score-sheet locking. | One tenant-safe control binds Tender/RFQ sources to the exact active reusable Evaluation Committee, policy/method/configuration/workflow lineage, required Chair/Secretary/voting roles, and source-specific appointments without duplicating shared controls. Appointment acceptance, versioned COI evidence, signed attendance, remote/hybrid evidence, server-derived quorum, phase-specific scorer eligibility, signed immutable score sheets, exact RFQ/NCT/legacy projection parity, and retained attempts are enforced. Recall requires evidence, row version, a fresh exact shared-workflow instance, independent approved processor/SOD, and creates a new attempt rather than overwriting history. Direct API/SQL bypass, tenant isolation, authorization, migration apply/rollback/reapply, 16 SQL hard stops, automation, browser/API smoke, cleanup, and unchanged PR/PO/receiving/inventory runtime are verified. |
| TDC-0209 | P0 | Done | Add recommendation approval and award-readiness gate. | One reusable tenant-safe decision re-derives every source-applicable evaluation and current locked score, unambiguous recommended bid/quote and supplier, supplier/prequalification eligibility, current verification/due-diligence outcome, exact policy/method/authority/shared-workflow/evidence lineage, actor, capability, SOD, source-integrity hash, concurrency, and idempotency. RFQ handoff, formal Tender award, Exceptional Sourcing award, and legacy `TenderAward` creation require the current exact `Ready` decision and `RecordAward` action; stale, recalled, foreign, mismatched, incomplete, ambiguous, unauthorized, or client-forged state is blocked and audited. Append-only SQL protection, tenant-safe API, dedicated shared history-first Tender/RFQ UI, migration lifecycle, automation, database probes, API/browser smoke, and cleanup are verified without changing general PR/PO/receiving/inventory runtime. |
| TDC-0210 | P0 | Done | Enforce evaluator-versus-award-approver SOD. | The existing `SOD-EVALUATOR-AWARD-APPROVER` hard stop is composed into award readiness and all four award boundaries. Tenant-safe direct and committee evaluator lineage, retained/recalled score attempts, exact authority/workflow approval actors, current readiness/hash, policy/rule, actor/roles, and correlation are derived server-side. A same-source evaluator acting as sole award approver is rejected before any decision/award mutation and audited; a distinct recorded non-evaluator approver prevents over-blocking. Internal relaxation inputs cannot bind from public JSON. Authenticated status API/shared UI fail closed, direct-route/tenant/bypass automation passes, no schema change is required, the current test database is verified, and API/browser smoke plus cleanup are complete. |
| TDC-0211 | P1 | Done | Implement successful/unsuccessful bidder communications and security returns. | One reusable tenant-safe register spans formal Tender, RFQ, and Exceptional Sourcing award families with server-derived successful/unsuccessful recipients; exact award/readiness/supplier/bid-or-quote lineage; approved immutable letter versions; dispatch, delivery, acknowledgement, standstill, appeal, and security release/return history; shared workflow/evidence/notification and `DEC-001` through `DEC-014` control events; authorization/SOD/concurrency/idempotency; ten-table append-only SQL protection; structured role-neutral API; dedicated shared internal and supplier-scoped external UI; focused/full automation, migration rollback/reapply and SQL probes, optimized build, API/browser/visual smoke, and exact cleanup. General PR/PO/receiving/inventory runtime and GHANEPS behavior remain unchanged. |
| TDC-0212 | P1 | Done | Add GHANEPS tender and award event exchange. | One configurable tenant-safe manual/API-ready exchange spans formal Tender, RFQ, and Exceptional Sourcing publication/award events. It derives the exact eligible source lifecycle and reference; requires one evidenced Published/effective `DEC-009` profile and unique applicable 17-property mapping; retains immutable source/configuration/mapping snapshots and hashes; versions controlled export/import payloads; and records submission success/failure/retry, acknowledgements, reconciliation/mismatch resolution, typed downloads, notifications, shared evidence, authorization/SOD, `DEC-001` through `DEC-014` control events, concurrency, and idempotency. Five-table append-only SQL protection, exact tenant/source/configuration foreign keys, direct SQL hard stops, structured role-neutral API, dedicated shared Tender/RFQ history/status UI, focused/full automation, migration rollback/reapply, 26 SQL probes repeated on both sides of the cycle, optimized build, API/browser/visual smoke, and exact cleanup are verified. Production provider transport/scheduling/reporting remains `TDC-0803`; general PR/PO/receiving/inventory runtime is unchanged. |

## Success Targets From TDC

| KPI | Target | Status | Implementation note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Manual data-entry errors | More than 80% reduction within 6 months | Not started | Define baseline sampling method and error taxonomy before go-live. |
| PR-to-PO cycle time | Reduce from 15 days to under 7 days | Not started | Measure from valid PR submission to approved/dispatched PO, excluding documented supplier waiting periods only if TDC approves. |
| APP compliance | 100% linked to approved APP | Not started | Approved exception must remain separately visible and must not silently count as compliant. |
| Stockout incidents | More than 60% reduction in first year | Not started | Define item/location stockout event and critical-item weighting. |
| Three-way match rate | More than 95% | Not started | Report matched invoices separately from approved exceptions. |
| Repeat audit findings | Zero | Not started | Link findings, corrective actions, owners, due dates, and retest evidence. |
| GHANEPS submissions | 100% on time | Not started | Use statutory due date, actual acknowledgement date, and resubmission status. |

Disposal, directed-task and mobile-document lists page until the requested number of authorized rows is collected rather than filtering only a pre-truncated page. Core/API builds complete with zero errors; full Core inventory passes `94/94`, focused tracking passes `9/9`, focused return/adjustment controls pass `9/9`, full API inventory passes `83/83`, and `git diff --check` passes. There is no entity/model, forward-schema, controller contract or frontend change, so no migration, database apply or browser change is required and the accepted configured evidence remains applicable. The exact timed-out EF/build process trees and compiler worker were terminated, the protected dashboard/team checkout remains untouched, and no task-owned runtime is left running. |

## Tracker Maintenance

- Start every implementation slice by selecting explicit `TDC-*` task IDs.
- Update task status when work starts; do not wait until the end of a large batch.
- Record migrations, tests, API routes, UI routes, and runtime evidence in the Verification Log.
- Keep `Current Coverage And Gap Matrix` synchronized when implementation changes the baseline.
- Do not remove statutory requirements from the tracker. Record an approved scope decision with evidence when TDC changes or defers a requirement.
- Do not mark a phase complete until matching backend, frontend, permissions, migration, reporting, and acceptance tests are present.
